#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§ROUTING-REGISTER-1 (EXP-2, the FINDING half) — write the wire, no gradient.

Pre-reg: mementum/knowledge/explore/write-not-train-ternary-routing-deltas.md
§ROUTING-REGISTER-1 (FROZEN s304, Michael-approved). Can the operand→capital
linker be WRITTEN (closed-form, no gradient, no calibration loop) as a ternary
bind-plate on the frozen base, installing a WIRE (generalizes to held-out
landmarks AND held-out countries)?

`construct` went INERT (byte-identical to base) because it wrote the MAGNITUDE
register: a continuous product-keyed neuron with a calibrated gain that throttled
to ≈0.3. The country key FIRED (s294: the landmark's latent country-ness triggers
the whitened filter); the throttled value write never installed the edge. Fix:
keep the MEASURED key as a faithful address, write the value in the ROUTING
register — ternary sign, register-matched full strength (S = median native
down_proj column norm at L23), NO gain calibration.

Write recipe (FROZEN, no gradient): at install layer L23 (INSTALL_DEPTH 0.65 * 36;
Qwen3-4B = 36 layers, band L22-L29), append one FFN neuron per country c (all 16;
the sum of key-bind-value realized as parallel FFN neurons; the bind is the FFN
key->value neuron structure, NOT literal circular convolution):
  address (gate/up rows) = measured whitened country filter k_c (build_keys,
    shared-Sigma; normalized gate=(4/ref)k, up=(1/ref)k, the proven firing rule)
  content (down col)     = S * ternary(v_c)/norm(ternary(v_c)) ; v_c = capital
    unembed direction; ternary = per-element TWN {-1,0,+1} thr 0.7; S = median
    native down_proj column L2-norm at L23 (host-register scale, NOT a tuned target)

Arms (deterministic write; re-scored on the frozen s303 gate-0 valid cells):
  base             : floor (0.200 / 0.125 / 0.545).
  routing_write    : the ternary bind-plate, all 16 countries.
  routing_shuffle  : deranged capital values (v_c → v_{π(c)}), same keys+S+sparsity
                     — the null (λ yardstick); must fail. ≥3 derangement seeds.
  construct_lookup : frozen materialized-view null (loaded), G2 baseline.

Gates (verbum.dsp paired-perm 10k, primaries Bonferroni alpha/3):
  G1 WIRE       : routing_write > base, flip on B1 AND B2.
  G2 NOT-LOOKUP : routing_write > construct_lookup on B2.
  G3 SPECIFICITY: routing_write > routing_shuffle on held-out (B1+B2).
  G5 SURVIVE    : innocent CE <= 2% rel base; native g/h within 0.10 abs.
Reports (advisory): achieved capital-logit boost on country frames (did the write
  LAND vs construct's 0.3 throttle?); trits/bits/sparsity (λ smallest); per-country
  key separation own_ref - inn_max (attribute an INERT verdict: weak-write vs
  no-routing).
Verdicts: WRITE-SUFFICES (G1∧G2∧G3∧G5 → thesis confirmed, never train the parent)
  / WRITE-DEGRADES (G1, ¬G3 or ¬G2) / WRITE-INERT (¬G1 → gradient-finds/
  ternary-stores) / HOST-DAMAGED (¬G5).

Cadence: --validate (no model) → smoke (--n-cells, mechanics only) → Michael GO
→ run → frozen scoring.

License: MIT (`λ provenance`).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict, is_dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(_HERE) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))
_WRAP = _HERE.parents[1] / "wrapper"
if str(_WRAP) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_WRAP))

import writeback_compile as wb  # noqa: E402  (module reuse, no fork)
from holo_frag import _json_safe  # noqa: E402

from verbum.dsp import gate, paired_permutation  # noqa: E402

SPLITS = wb.SPLITS
TERN_THR = 0.7
LOG2_3 = float(np.log2(3.0))


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Ternarize a VALUE vector (per-element TWN) + derangement
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def ternarize_vec(v: np.ndarray, thr: float = TERN_THR):
    """Per-element TWN ternary on a vector. Returns (t, mask, gamma)."""
    absv = np.abs(v)
    theta = thr * absv.mean()
    mask = absv > theta
    gamma = float(absv[mask].mean()) if mask.any() else 0.0
    t = np.sign(v) * mask * gamma
    return t.astype(np.float32), mask, gamma


def unit(v: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    n = np.linalg.norm(v)
    return v / n if n > 1e-12 else v


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Frozen scoring + verdict (pure; --validate exercises planted worlds)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def _g(a, b, rng, alpha, name):
    a = np.asarray(a, float)
    b = np.asarray(b, float)
    return gate(float(np.mean(a - b)), paired_permutation(a, b, rng),
                "greater", alpha, name=name)


def score(acc: dict, ce: dict, gh: dict, rng, alpha: float) -> dict:
    a3 = alpha / 3.0
    arm, base = "routing_write", "base"
    r = {}
    g1 = {}
    for sp in ("B1", "B2"):
        gg = _g(acc[arm][sp], acc[base][sp], rng, a3, f"G1-{sp}")
        g1[sp] = {"gate": gg, "flip": bool(acc[arm][sp].mean()
                                           > acc[base][sp].mean())}
    r["G1"] = bool(all(g1[sp]["gate"].verdict and g1[sp]["flip"]
                       for sp in ("B1", "B2")))
    r["G1_detail"] = g1
    g2 = _g(acc[arm]["B2"], acc["construct_lookup"]["B2"], rng, a3, "G2-B2")
    r["G2"] = bool(g2.verdict)
    r["G2_detail"] = g2
    held = np.concatenate([acc[arm]["B1"], acc[arm]["B2"]])
    held_s = np.concatenate([acc["routing_shuffle"]["B1"],
                             acc["routing_shuffle"]["B2"]])
    g3 = _g(held, held_s, rng, a3, "G3-heldout")
    r["G3"] = bool(g3.verdict)
    r["G3_detail"] = g3
    ce_ok = ce[arm] <= ce[base] * 1.02
    g_ok = gh[arm][0] >= gh[base][0] - 0.10
    h_ok = gh[arm][1] >= gh[base][1] - 0.10
    r["G5"] = bool(ce_ok and g_ok and h_ok)
    r["G5_detail"] = {"ce": ce[arm], "ce_base": ce[base],
                      "g_acc": gh[arm][0], "h_acc": gh[arm][1]}
    r["held_up"] = bool(held.mean() > np.concatenate(
        [acc[base]["B1"], acc[base]["B2"]]).mean())
    return r


def verdict_of(gate0_ok: bool, r: dict) -> str:
    if not gate0_ok:
        return "VOID (gate-0)"
    if not r["G5"]:
        return "HOST-DAMAGED"
    if r["G1"] and r["G2"] and r["G3"]:
        return "WRITE-SUFFICES"
    if r["G1"] and (not r["G3"] or not r["G2"]):
        return "WRITE-DEGRADES"
    if not r["G1"]:
        return "WRITE-INERT"
    return "inconclusive"


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# --validate (no model)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:
    ok = True
    print("── §ROUTING-REGISTER-1 --validate (no model) ──")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    # 1. ternarize_vec: sane sparsity, sign preserved
    v = rng.normal(size=2560)
    t, mask, gamma = ternarize_vec(v)
    sign_ok = float((np.sign(t[mask]) == np.sign(v[mask])).mean())
    spars = 1.0 - mask.mean()
    good = mask.any() and 0.0 < spars < 1.0 and sign_ok == 1.0 and gamma > 0
    print(f"[V] ternarize_vec: sparsity {spars:.2f} sign_ok {sign_ok:.2f} "
          f"gamma {gamma:.3f} {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
    ok &= good

    # 2. neuron surgery equivalence (tiny SwiGLU) — append/restore correctness
    import torch
    import torch.nn.functional as F
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    dm, ff = 16, 32
    gp = torch.nn.Linear(dm, ff, bias=False)
    up = torch.nn.Linear(dm, ff, bias=False)
    dn = torch.nn.Linear(ff, dm, bias=False)

    def mlp(x):
        return dn(F.silu(gp(x)) * up(x))

    key = unit(rng.normal(size=dm).astype(np.float32))
    val = rng.normal(size=dm).astype(np.float32)
    ref = 2.0
    x_on = torch.tensor(ref * key)
    x_off = x_on - float(x_on @ torch.tensor(key)) * torch.tensor(key)
    base_on, base_off = mlp(x_on), mlp(x_off)
    kt = torch.tensor(key)
    with torch.no_grad():
        gp.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.cat(
            [gp.weight, ((4.0 / ref) * kt)[None, :]]))
        up.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.cat(
            [up.weight, ((1.0 / ref) * kt)[None, :]]))
        dn.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.cat(
            [dn.weight, torch.tensor(val)[:, None]], dim=1))
    r = float(x_on @ kt)
    want = base_on + F.silu(torch.tensor(4.0 * r / ref)) * (r / ref) \
        * torch.tensor(val)
    with torch.no_grad():
        e_on = float((mlp(x_on) - want).abs().max())
        e_off = float((mlp(x_off) - base_off).abs().max())
    good = e_on < 1e-4 and e_off < 1e-4
    print(f"[V] surgery: on-err {e_on:.2e} off-err {e_off:.2e} "
          f"{'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
    ok &= good

    # 3. derangement no fixed point
    d = wb.derangement(sorted(wb.BANK), np.random.default_rng(1))
    good = all(k != x for k, x in d.items()) and set(d.values()) == set(wb.BANK)
    print(f"[V] derangement: {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
    ok &= good

    # 4. S = median native column norm
    w = rng.normal(size=(dm, ff))
    s = float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(w, axis=0)))
    good = s > 0 and abs(s - np.median(np.linalg.norm(w, axis=0))) < 1e-9
    print(f"[V] S median col-norm {s:.3f} {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
    ok &= good

    # 5. verdict planted worlds
    def world(name, want, wr, base, shuf, lookup, ce_bad=False, gh_bad=False):
        rngw = np.random.default_rng(hash(name) & 0xFFFF)

        def arr(p, n=64):
            return (rngw.random(n) < p).astype(float)

        acc = {
            "base": {"TRAIN": arr(base[0]), "B1": arr(base[1]),
                     "B2": arr(base[2])},
            "routing_write": {"TRAIN": arr(wr[0]), "B1": arr(wr[1]),
                              "B2": arr(wr[2])},
            "routing_shuffle": {"TRAIN": arr(shuf[0]), "B1": arr(shuf[1]),
                                "B2": arr(shuf[2])},
            "construct_lookup": {"TRAIN": arr(lookup[0]), "B1": arr(lookup[1]),
                                 "B2": arr(lookup[2])},
        }
        ce = {a: (1.10 if (ce_bad and a == "routing_write") else 1.0)
              for a in acc}
        gh = {a: ((0.5, 0.5) if (gh_bad and a == "routing_write")
                  else (0.95, 0.95)) for a in acc}
        r = score(acc, ce, gh, np.random.default_rng(3), alpha)
        v = verdict_of(True, r)
        hit = want in v
        print(f"[V] {name}-world -> {v} (want {want}) {'OK' if hit else 'FAIL'}")
        return hit

    ok &= world("suffices", "WRITE-SUFFICES",
                wr=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3),
                shuf=(.2, .12, .2), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))
    ok &= world("degrades", "WRITE-DEGRADES",
                wr=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3),
                shuf=(.9, .9, .92), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))
    ok &= world("inert", "WRITE-INERT",
                wr=(.2, .12, .3), base=(.2, .12, .3),
                shuf=(.2, .12, .28), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))
    ok &= world("host-damaged", "HOST-DAMAGED",
                wr=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3),
                shuf=(.2, .12, .2), lookup=(.27, .12, .35), ce_bad=True)

    print(f"\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──")
    return 0 if ok else 1


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Model path
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_model(args) -> int:
    import operand_multihop3 as mh3
    import torch
    import torch.nn.functional as F
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    dev = (args.device if (args.device != "mps"
                           or torch.backends.mps.is_available()) else "cpu")
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)
    if tok.pad_token is None:
        tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token
    tok.padding_side = "left"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype)).to(dev).eval()
    for p in model.parameters():
        p.requires_grad_(False)
    dec, _norm, lm_head = mh3.resolve_parts(model)
    n_layers = len(dec)
    li = round(wb.INSTALL_DEPTH * n_layers)
    out_dir = Path(args.out)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rec = Path(args.record_dir)

    def first_tid(w: str) -> int:
        return mh3.first_tid(tok, w)

    # ── valid cells + construct_lookup baseline from the FROZEN s303 record ──
    g0 = json.loads((rec / "gate0.json").read_text())
    gate0_ok = bool(g0["gate0_ok"])
    fields = ("landmark", "city", "country", "capital", "split")
    valid = [wb.Cell(**{k: c[k] for k in fields}) for c in g0["cells"]
             if c.get("g_ok") and c.get("h_ok") and c.get("cot_ok")]
    ns = {sp: sum(1 for c in valid if c.split == sp) for sp in SPLITS}
    res_frozen = json.loads((rec / "results.json").read_text())
    lookup_b2 = {x["landmark"]: x["correct"]
                 for x in res_frozen["arms"]["construct_lookup"]["seeds"][0]
                 if x["split"] == "B2"}
    print(f"[rr] {args.model_id} dev={dev} n_layers={n_layers} install=L{li} "
          f"valid={len(valid)} splits={ns} shuffle_seeds={args.seeds} "
          f"gate0_ok={gate0_ok}")

    if args.n_cells:                       # smoke cap (mechanics only)
        by = {sp: [c for c in valid if c.split == sp] for sp in SPLITS}
        valid = [c for sp in SPLITS for c in by[sp][:args.n_cells]]
        lookup_b2 = {c.landmark: lookup_b2.get(c.landmark, 0.0)
                     for c in valid if c.split == "B2"}
        print(f"[rr] SMOKE cap {args.n_cells}/split -> {len(valid)} cells")

    # ── union candidate set ──
    tid_map, drop = {}, set()
    for w in wb.union_words():
        t = first_tid(w)
        clash = [x for x, tt in tid_map.items() if tt == t]
        if clash:
            drop.add(w)
            drop.update(clash)
        tid_map[w] = t
    union = {w: tid_map[w] for w in sorted(set(wb.union_words()) - drop)}

    countries = sorted(wb.BANK)
    caps = sorted({cap for cap, _ in wb.BANK.values()})

    def logits_last(prompt: str) -> np.ndarray:
        ids = tok(prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            return model(**ids).logits[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()

    def argmax_union(lo):
        return max(union, key=lambda w: lo[union[w]])

    def margin(lo, truth):
        return float(lo[union[truth]]
                     - max(lo[union[w]] for w in union if w != truth))

    def eval_cells() -> list[dict]:
        rows = []
        for c in valid:
            lo = logits_last(wb.DIRECT_PROMPT.format(lm=c.landmark))
            arg = argmax_union(lo)
            rows.append({"landmark": c.landmark, "country": c.country,
                         "split": c.split, "truth": c.capital, "arg": arg,
                         "correct": float(wb.first_word(arg)
                                          == wb.first_word(c.capital)),
                         "margin": margin(lo, c.capital)})
        return rows

    def ce_innocents() -> float:
        tot, n = 0.0, 0
        for t in wb.CE_TEXTS:
            ids = tok(t, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
            with torch.no_grad():
                lo = model(**ids).logits
            lp = F.log_softmax(lo[0, :-1].float(), dim=-1)
            tgt = ids.input_ids[0, 1:]
            tot += float(-lp[torch.arange(len(tgt)), tgt].sum())
            n += len(tgt)
        return tot / max(n, 1)

    def gh_accs():
        g = [max(countries, key=lambda w: logits_last(
            wb.G_QUERY_PREFIX + wb.G_QUERY.format(lm=c.landmark))[first_tid(w)])
            == c.country for c in valid]
        h = [wb.first_word(max(caps, key=lambda w: logits_last(
            wb.CAP_PREFIX + wb.CAP_QUERY.format(x=co))[first_tid(w)]))
            == wb.first_word(wb.BANK[co][0]) for co in sorted(wb.BANK)]
        return float(np.mean(g)), float(np.mean(h))

    # ── post-norm capture + whitened country keys (build_keys, re-impl) ──
    def capture_postnorm(prompts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        vecs = []
        for p in prompts:
            store = {}
            hnd = dec[li].post_attention_layernorm.register_forward_hook(
                lambda m, i, o, s=store: s.__setitem__("v", o))
            ids = tok(p, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
            with torch.no_grad():
                model(**ids)
            hnd.remove()
            vecs.append(store["v"][0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy())
        return np.stack(vecs)

    def build_keys(specs: dict[str, list[str]]) -> dict:
        inn_prompts = list(wb.PROSE_INNOCENTS) + [
            wb.DIRECT_PROMPT.format(lm=nc) for nc in wb.NONCE_CANDS[:3]]
        inn = capture_postnorm(inn_prompts)
        owns = {name: capture_postnorm(ps) for name, ps in specs.items()}
        pop = np.vstack([*owns.values(), inn])
        mu = pop.mean(axis=0)
        xc = pop - mu
        cov = (xc.T @ xc) / max(len(pop) - 1, 1)
        d = cov.shape[0]
        cov += args.whiten_eps * (np.trace(cov) / d) * np.eye(d)
        keys = {}
        seps = []
        for name, own in owns.items():
            k = np.linalg.solve(cov, own.mean(axis=0) - mu)
            k = unit(k)
            keys[name] = {"k": k, "ref": float(np.mean(own @ k)),
                          "inn_max": float(np.max(inn @ k))}
            seps.append(keys[name]["ref"] - keys[name]["inn_max"])
        print(f"[rr] keys({len(keys)}): own-inn separation min {min(seps):.2f} "
              f"median {float(np.median(seps)):.2f}")
        return keys

    def unembed_dir(word: str) -> np.ndarray:
        v = lm_head.weight[first_tid(word)].float().cpu().numpy()
        return unit(v)

    # ── neuron surgery (append/restore; validated pattern) ──
    mlp = dec[li].mlp
    ff_orig = mlp.gate_proj.weight.shape[0]

    def append_neurons(neurons):
        """neurons: list of (k_unit, ref, down_col_vec)."""
        wd = mlp.gate_proj.weight.dtype
        g_rows = torch.stack([torch.tensor((4.0 / ref) * k, dtype=wd)
                              for (k, ref, _) in neurons]).to(dev)
        u_rows = torch.stack([torch.tensor((1.0 / ref) * k, dtype=wd)
                              for (k, ref, _) in neurons]).to(dev)
        d_cols = torch.stack([torch.tensor(v, dtype=wd)
                              for (_, _, v) in neurons], dim=1).to(dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            mlp.gate_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                torch.cat([mlp.gate_proj.weight[:ff_orig], g_rows]),
                requires_grad=False)
            mlp.up_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                torch.cat([mlp.up_proj.weight[:ff_orig], u_rows]),
                requires_grad=False)
            mlp.down_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                torch.cat([mlp.down_proj.weight[:, :ff_orig], d_cols], dim=1),
                requires_grad=False)
        mlp.gate_proj.out_features = ff_orig + len(neurons)
        mlp.up_proj.out_features = ff_orig + len(neurons)
        mlp.down_proj.in_features = ff_orig + len(neurons)

    def restore_neurons():
        with torch.no_grad():
            mlp.gate_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                mlp.gate_proj.weight[:ff_orig].contiguous(),
                requires_grad=False)
            mlp.up_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                mlp.up_proj.weight[:ff_orig].contiguous(), requires_grad=False)
            mlp.down_proj.weight = torch.nn.Parameter(
                mlp.down_proj.weight[:, :ff_orig].contiguous(),
                requires_grad=False)
        mlp.gate_proj.out_features = ff_orig
        mlp.up_proj.out_features = ff_orig
        mlp.down_proj.in_features = ff_orig

    # ── register scale S = median native down_proj column L2-norm at L23 ──
    dn_w = mlp.down_proj.weight[:, :ff_orig].float().cpu().numpy()
    S = float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(dn_w, axis=0)))
    print(f"[rr] register scale S = median native down col-norm = {S:.4f}")

    # ── build country keys (from country-name frames) + capital values ──
    country_specs = {c: [f.format(x=c) for f in wb.CC_FRAMES]
                     for c in countries}
    keys = build_keys(country_specs)
    tern_val = {}      # ternary unit capital direction per country
    trit_report = {"trits": 0, "params": 0}
    for c in countries:
        t, mask, _ = ternarize_vec(unembed_dir(wb.BANK[c][0]))
        tern_val[c] = unit(t)             # unit ternary direction
        trit_report["trits"] += int(mask.sum())
        trit_report["params"] += int(t.size)

    # advisory: capital-logit boost on country frames (did the write land?)
    def boost_on_country_frames(cap_of: dict) -> float:
        neurons = [(keys[c]["k"], keys[c]["ref"], S * tern_val[cap_of[c]])
                   for c in countries]
        base_vals, plate_vals = [], []
        frames = {c: [f.format(x=c) for f in wb.CC_FRAMES] for c in countries}
        for c in countries:
            for p in frames[c]:
                base_vals.append(logits_last(p)[first_tid(wb.BANK[c][0])])
        append_neurons(neurons)
        for c in countries:
            for p in frames[c]:
                plate_vals.append(logits_last(p)[first_tid(wb.BANK[c][0])])
        restore_neurons()
        return float(np.mean(np.array(plate_vals) - np.array(base_vals)))

    # ── eval an arm given a country->capital map (identity = routing_write) ──
    def eval_write(cap_of: dict):
        neurons = [(keys[c]["k"], keys[c]["ref"], S * tern_val[cap_of[c]])
                   for c in countries]
        append_neurons(neurons)
        rows = eval_cells()
        ce = ce_innocents()
        gh = gh_accs()
        restore_neurons()
        return rows, ce, gh

    # ══ run arms ══
    print("[rr] ── base ──")
    base_rows = eval_cells()
    base_ce = ce_innocents()
    base_gh = gh_accs()
    for sp in SPLITS:
        print(f"    {sp}: acc "
              f"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in base_rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}")

    print("[rr] ── routing_write ──")
    ident = {c: c for c in countries}
    wr_rows, wr_ce, wr_gh = eval_write(ident)
    landed = boost_on_country_frames(ident)
    print(f"[rr] achieved capital-logit boost on country frames = {landed:.3f} "
          f"(construct throttled to ~0.3)")
    for sp in SPLITS:
        print(f"    {sp}: acc "
              f"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in wr_rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}")

    print(f"[rr] ── routing_shuffle ({args.seeds} derangement seeds) ──")
    shuf_seed_rows, shuf_ce, shuf_gh = [], [], []
    for s in range(args.seeds):
        dc = wb.derangement(countries, np.random.default_rng(1000 + s))
        rows, ce, gh = eval_write(dc)          # cap_of[c] = BANK-cap of dc[c]
        # dc maps country->country; capital value = tern_val[dc[c]]
        shuf_seed_rows.append(rows)
        shuf_ce.append(ce)
        shuf_gh.append(gh)
        for sp in SPLITS:
            print(f"    seed {s} {sp}: acc "
                  f"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}")

    arms = {
        "base": {"seeds": [base_rows], "ce": base_ce, "gh": base_gh},
        "routing_write": {"seeds": [wr_rows], "ce": wr_ce, "gh": wr_gh,
                          "boost": landed},
        "routing_shuffle": {"seeds": shuf_seed_rows,
                            "ce": float(np.mean(shuf_ce)),
                            "gh": tuple(np.mean(shuf_gh, axis=0))},
        "construct_lookup": {"b2": lookup_b2},
    }

    # ══ frozen scoring ══
    order = {sp: [c.landmark for c in valid if c.split == sp] for sp in SPLITS}

    def acc_arrays(label) -> dict:
        per = {}
        for sp in SPLITS:
            mat = []
            for rows in arms[label]["seeds"]:
                bym = {r["landmark"]: r["correct"] for r in rows
                       if r["split"] == sp}
                mat.append([bym[lm] for lm in order[sp]])
            per[sp] = np.mean(np.array(mat), axis=0)
        return per

    acc = {a: acc_arrays(a) for a in ("base", "routing_write",
                                      "routing_shuffle")}
    acc["construct_lookup"] = {
        "B2": np.array([lookup_b2[lm] for lm in order["B2"]]),
        "B1": np.zeros(len(order["B1"])),
        "TRAIN": np.zeros(len(order["TRAIN"])),
    }
    ce = {"base": base_ce, "routing_write": wr_ce}
    gh = {"base": base_gh, "routing_write": wr_gh}
    r = score(acc, ce, gh, np.random.default_rng(args.seed + 999), args.alpha)
    v = verdict_of(gate0_ok, r)

    stats = {"trits": trit_report["trits"], "params": trit_report["params"],
             "bits": trit_report["trits"] * LOG2_3,
             "sparsity": 1.0 - trit_report["trits"]
             / max(trit_report["params"], 1),
             "boost": landed, "S": S,
             "key_sep_min": float(min(keys[c]["ref"] - keys[c]["inn_max"]
                                      for c in countries)),
             "key_sep_median": float(np.median(
                 [keys[c]["ref"] - keys[c]["inn_max"] for c in countries]))}
    anchor = {sp: {"routing_write": float(acc["routing_write"][sp].mean()),
                   "routing_shuffle": float(acc["routing_shuffle"][sp].mean()),
                   "base": float(acc["base"][sp].mean())} for sp in SPLITS}

    print(f"\n[rr] ════ VERDICT: {v} ════")
    print(f"  G1={r['G1']} G2={r['G2']} G3={r['G3']} G5={r['G5']}")
    print(f"  boost={landed:.3f} key_sep_min={stats['key_sep_min']:.2f} "
          f"trits={stats['trits']} sparsity={stats['sparsity']:.3f}")
    for sp in SPLITS:
        print(f"  {sp}: base {anchor[sp]['base']:.3f} write "
              f"{anchor[sp]['routing_write']:.3f} shuffle "
              f"{anchor[sp]['routing_shuffle']:.3f}")

    def _degate(o):
        if is_dataclass(o) and not isinstance(o, type):
            return asdict(o)
        if isinstance(o, dict):
            return {k: _degate(x) for k, x in o.items()}
        if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)):
            return [_degate(x) for x in o]
        return o

    scoring = {"gates": r, "verdict": v, "stats": stats, "anchor": anchor}
    payload = {"model_id": args.model_id, "config": vars(args),
               "install_layer": li, "gate0": {"ok": gate0_ok, "splits": ns},
               "arms": arms, "scoring": scoring}
    (out_dir / "results.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(_json_safe(_degate(payload)), indent=2))
    print(f"[rr] wrote {out_dir}/results.json")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--model-id", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="mps")
    ap.add_argument("--dtype", default="bfloat16",
                    choices=["float32", "bfloat16"])
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=3,
                    help="derangement seeds for the routing_shuffle null")
    ap.add_argument("--whiten-eps", type=float, default=0.1)
    ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--n-cells", type=int, default=0,
                    help="smoke: cap cells per split (mechanics only)")
    ap.add_argument("--record-dir",
                    default="results/writeback-compile/qwen3-4b")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/routing-register/qwen3-4b")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.validate:
        return run_validate(args.alpha)
    return run_model(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

### Thinking

I'm seeing the pattern now—for fast_plate.py, I need to implement a materialization scan that's fundamentally different from routing_register. This scan captures activations across every layer during training on landmark direct prompts, builds per-country landmark prototypes, and uses leave-one-landmark-out decodability to identify the optimal layer, then sets up the plate as a forward hook rather than an app.

Now I'm working through the hook mechanics carefully: I need to read the post-attention-layernorm activation at the selected layer L* (which is where the recognition keys are built from), but write the reinject vector into the residual stream by hooking the decoder layer output and modifying it at the last token position.

The tricky part is coordinating reads and writes within the same layer's forward pass. I can use two hooks—one on the post-attention-layernorm to capture the activation, and one on the decoder layer itself to modify the output—since the layernorm hook fires first during the layer's forward, then the layer hook fires after the layer completes, giving me access to the captured activation to compute and inject the reinject vector.

Now I'm realizing the geometry mismatch: the reinject vector lives in the post-attention-layernorm space at layer L*, but I'm adding it to the residual (layer output) space. These are different geometric spaces, so I need to figure out how to properly transform or align them. is means I'd capture v_c from the residual stream at layer L*, then reinject it back into the residual at the same layer. This keeps everything in one consistent space—scan, recognition, and reinject all operate on the same representation, which is cleaner than mixing post-attention-layernorm and residual spaces. Each layer's forward hook will both read the residual output and inject the country prototype back into that same residual before it propagates downstream. This keeps everything in a single consistent space and lets the h-hop layer read the modified residual. Now I need to check how Qwen3 structures its layer outputs to make sure I'm extracting the right tensor. There's a mismatch between how I'm measuring decodability in the scan versus how recognition actually works at test time. The scan uses landmark prototypes with leave-one-out cross-validation to determine if country information is decodable from landmark activations, but recognition relies on name-frame keys. If the country signal materializes differently in landmark geometry versus name geometry, the scan could indicate decodability where recognition would actually fail.

I deliberately chose name-frame keys in the pre-registration because they cover all 16 countries including B2, whereas landmark prototypes only exist for the 8 training countries. So for B2 countries, name-keys are the only option. This design tension is intentional—the scan tells me the country IS encoded in landmark geometry, but recognition via name-keys tests whether that signal aligns well enough in name geometry at L* to actually recognize it. When name-keys don't align, recognition fails and fast_plate stays inert, which is exactly what the landmark-versus-name cosine advisory would explain.

I'm tempted to use landmark prototypes for training countries where we have them to get better performance, but the pre-registration froze name-frame keys uniformly across all 16 countries. The whole point is that B2 must work the same way as B1—wire-based, not lookup-based—and name-keys are the only uniform option. I'll implement exactly as frozen.

Now I'm redefining the country prototype v_c as the mean of name-frame activations for country c at L*. Both the recognition key and reinject value come from the same name-frame activations at L*, so I can adapt build_keys to work in residual space and also extract the raw mean for the reinject. For the confidence floor, I'm using the innocent projection maximum that build_keys already computes per key—that becomes the threshold for country c.

Now I need to design the concrete scan across layers. For each training country, I'll capture residuals at every layer using the training landmarks from valid cells, then compute per-layer country prototypes as the mean of each country's landmark activations. I'll validate decodability through leave-one-landmark-out classification—checking if each landmark gets correctly classified to its true country using whitened prototypes built from the other landmarks. To establish a null distribution, I'll shuffle country labels across landmarks and recompute the LOO accuracy multiple times.

The gate checks whether the best layer's decodability significantly exceeds the null distribution at significance level α using a directional test. But with only 16 training points (8 countries × 2 landmarks), LOO decodability is very coarse, and whitening a ~2560-dimensional covariance from so few samples becomes numerically unstable. So nearest-prototype classification in whitened space is unreliable here—I should switch to a simpler decodability metric like cosine similarity instead.

I'm reconsidering the approach: use raw or just centered space without whitening for the layer scan, or leverage the name-frame prototypes as references since there are more samples per country there. The core goal is finding which layer best decodes the landmark-inferred country in a way that's robust to small sample sizes.

For each landmark activation at layer L, I'd compute cosine similarity to each country's name-frame prototype and pick the argmax to check against the true country. This directly measures whether landmark activations align with the correct country's name-geometry at that layer—which is exactly what recognition depends on. Using name prototypes as references is more principled since they're well-sampled. This makes decodability and recognition nearly the same computation, which feels consistent and honest: L* becomes the layer where landmark activations best align with name-frame country prototypes, which is precisely where name-key recognition will succeed.

Now I need to decide between whitened keys versus cosine similarity. For the scan, I should use the same recognition procedure I'll apply at test time: whitened name-keys built from the name-frames plus innocents at layer L, then classify each landmark activation by taking the argmax of its dot product with those keys. The whitening covariance comes from a large sample pool of name-frames across all countries plus innocents.

For each layer L, I build the keys, then for every TRAIN landmark activation I compute which country it recognizes as via argmax, and measure decodability as the fraction of correct recognitions. I pick L* as the layer with highest decodability in the lower two-thirds, and the null uses scrambled country labels to control for chance.

    "France": ("Paris", [("Mont Saint-Michel", "Avranches", "B2"),

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Cell:
    landmark: str
    city: str
    country: str
    capital: str
    split: str


def all_cells() -> list[Cell]:
    out = []
    for c, (cap, lms) in BANK.items():
        for (lm, city, split) in lms:
            out.append(Cell(lm, city, c, cap, split))
    return out


def first_word(s: str) -> str:
    return s.split()[0] if s else s


def fold(s: str) -> str:
    """Accent-fold for substring checks (s302 gate-0 amendment: the host
    writes 'Brasília'; the bank is ASCII — measurement register, not task)."""
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode()


def union_words() -> list[str]:
    caps = {cap for cap, _ in BANK.values()}
    countries = set(BANK)
    cities = {city for _, lms in BANK.values() for (_, city, _) in lms}
    return sorted(caps | countries | cities)


def derangement(items: list[str], rng: np.random.Generator) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Permutation with no fixed point."""
    n = len(items)
    while True:
        p = rng.permutation(n)
        if not np.any(p == np.arange(n)):
            return {items[i]: items[p[i]] for i in range(n)}


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Frozen scoring + verdict (pure; --validate exercises planted worlds)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def _g(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray, rng, alpha: float, name: str):
    return gate(float(np.mean(np.asarray(a) - np.asarray(b))),
                paired_permutation(np.asarray(a), np.asarray(b), rng),
                "greater", alpha, name=name)

def first_tid(tok, w):
    return tok(" " + w, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[0]


def cap_hook(store, li):
    def hook(_m, _i, out):
        h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
        store[li] = h.detach().float().cpu().numpy()
    return hook


def add_hook_at(vec_t, pos):
    def hook(_m, _i, out):
        h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
        if 0 <= pos < h.shape[1]:
            h[0, pos, :] = h[0, pos, :] + vec_t.to(h.dtype)
        return out
    return hook


def resolve_parts(model):
    """(decoder-layers, final-norm, lm_head) across architectures (dense + hybrid)."""
    inner = model.model
    lm = inner if hasattr(inner, "layers") else inner.language_model
    return lm.layers, lm.norm, model.lm_head

# landmarks; a mix so d_cc is generic "a country is present", not one nation)
CC_CALIB = ["France", "Germany", "Japan", "Brazil", "Kenya", "Canada",
            "Portugal", "Thailand", "Norway", "Chile"]

# §3a-whitened (s295): multi-lighting country frames + innocents for the
# whitened matched-filter detector (SuperBake whitening law: raw mean keys
# measure the shared question subspace; Σ must include innocents).
CC_FRAMES = [CC_FRAME,
             "The treaty was signed by {x}",
             "Many travelers dream of visiting {x}"]
PROSE_INNOCENTS = [
    "The recipe calls for two cups of flour",

    "The meeting was rescheduled to next week",

def whitened_filter(own: np.ndarray, innocents: np.ndarray, eps: float):
    """SuperBake-law matched filter: k = Sigma_sh^-1(mean_own - mu_pop),
    population = own + innocents; Sigma_sh = Sigma + eps*(tr/D)*I (ridge, n << D).
    Returns (k, mu, theta, ref): theta = max innocent response (clearance
    floor), ref = mean own response. Pure numpy; --validate exercises it."""
    pop = np.vstack([own, innocents])
    mu = pop.mean(axis=0)
    xc = pop - mu
    cov = (xc.T @ xc) / max(len(pop) - 1, 1)
    d = cov.shape[0]
    cov += eps * (np.trace(cov) / d) * np.eye(d)
    k = np.linalg.solve(cov, own.mean(axis=0) - mu)
    own_r = (own - mu) @ k
    inn_r = (innocents - mu) @ k
    return k, mu, float(np.max(inn_r)), float(np.mean(own_r))


def detector_gain(r: np.ndarray, k: np.ndarray, mu: np.ndarray,
                  theta: float, ref: float, cap: float) -> float:
    """Unified gain: clip((proj - theta)/(ref - theta), 0, cap). Raw: theta=0."""
    proj = float(np.dot(r - mu, k))
    return float(np.clip((proj - theta) / max(ref - theta, 1e-9), 0.0, cap))

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"§P-FAST-PLATE — the last construction door: in-forward cleanup-and-reinject.\n\nPre-reg: mementum/knowledge/explore/write-not-train-ternary-routing-deltas.md\n§P-FAST-PLATE (FROZEN s305, Michael-approved; mechanization = cleanup-and-reinject\nover the delta-rule capital-relay). The s304 triangulation closed STATIC\nconstruction in BOTH registers (construct magnitude INERT + routing_write routing\nINERT; only gd_cd gradient WIRE). The one untested door (§5c of\nholographic-reduction-machine.md): a plate written BY the forward pass — the only\nmechanism with access to the intermediate the pass materializes.\n\nrouting_write read at L23 in NAMED-country geometry (where the one-shot landmark\nprompt does NOT materialize the country) and wrote the CAPITAL directly → INERT.\nP-FAST-PLATE inverts both moves: READ where the country is materialized-from-\nlandmark, argmax-COLLAPSE to the nearest of 16 name-frame country keys\n(confidence-floored), REINJECT the country in named geometry, and let the host's\nOWN h-hop produce the capital. Two static-plate-impossible operations: (1)\nnonlinear winner-take-all collapse (the s300 pin / §4 internal-collapse organ);\n(2) read-geometry != write-geometry. The plate stores only COUNTRY (not capital)\n→ B2 (held-country) generalizes free (host knows all capitals via native h-hop).\n\nMATERIALIZATION SCAN — read-only pre-gate M (TRAIN-only, FROZEN, hard stop):\n  on TRAIN landmark DIRECT prompts, per layer L build shared-Sigma name-frame\n  country keys, classify each TRAIN landmark activation (argmax over 16 keys),\n  decodability(L) = mean(pred == true country). Null = permuted-label accuracy,\n  max over the candidate layers (multiple-comparison safe). M passes iff the best\n  layer beats the null at alpha.\n    ¬M -> STILL-EXTERNAL-BY-MEASUREMENT (the country is never linearly\n          materialized one-shot; the s295 exhaustion law is MECHANICAL). STOP.\n    M  -> L* = highest-decodability layer in the LOWER 2/3 of the stack (h-hop\n          room downstream); ties -> lowest layer.\n\nTHE PLATE (single forward hook on dec[L*], all positions, residual space):\n  recognize c* = argmax_c (a - mu) @ k_c ; fire iff proj > inn_max_{c*} (floor,\n  built from PROSE_INNOCENTS + NONCE -> no fire on innocents, protects F5) ;\n  reinject S * proto_{c*} into the residual, proto_c = unit(mu_own_c - mu_pop)\n  (population-centered named prototype = the country-specific named direction),\n  S = median native down_proj column L2-norm at L* (register-matched, no loop).\n\nArms (re-scored on the FROZEN 53 gate-0 cells from the s303 record):\n  base              : floor (0.200 / 0.125 / 0.545).\n  fast_plate        : the cleanup-reinject (hard argmax collapse + confidence floor).\n  fast_plate_shuffle: recognize c*, reinject proto_{derange(c*)} — the null\n                      (lambda yardstick); matched strength/geometry. >=3 seeds.\n  static_reinject   : soft always-on Sum_c softmax(a.k_c) * S * proto_c (same\n                      read/write geometry, NO hard collapse, NO floor) —\n                      collapse-isolation.\n  construct_lookup  : inherited materialized-view null (loaded), F2 baseline.\n\nGates (verbum.dsp paired-perm 10k, primaries Bonferroni alpha/3):\n  F1 WIRE       : fast_plate > base, flip on B1 AND B2.\n  F2 NOT-LOOKUP : fast_plate > construct_lookup on B2.\n  F3 SPECIFICITY: fast_plate > fast_plate_shuffle on held-out (B1+B2).\n  F5 SURVIVE    : innocent CE <= 2% rel base; native g/h within 0.10 abs.\nReports (advisory, NOT gates): collapse_delta (fast_plate - static_reinject on\n  held-out; the COLLAPSE-LOAD-BEARING vs GEOMETRY-SUFFICES fork), decodability\n  curve + L*, landmark-vs-name cosine at L*, TRAIN recognition acc, reinject_landed\n  (did the write move the correct-capital logit? weak-write vs no-routing).\nVerdicts: STILL-EXTERNAL-BY-MEASUREMENT (¬M) / FAST-PLATE-WIRES\n  (+COLLAPSE-LOAD-BEARING | +GEOMETRY-SUFFICES) / FAST-PLATE-INERT (M∧¬F1) /\n  UNSPECIFIC (F1∧¬(F2∧F3)) / HOST-DAMAGED (¬F5).\n\nCadence: --validate (no model) -> smoke (--n-cells, mechanics only) -> Michael GO\n-> run -> frozen scoring.\n\nLicense: MIT (`λ provenance`).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom dataclasses import asdict, is_dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nif str(_HERE) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))\n_WRAP = _HERE.parents[1] / \"wrapper\"\nif str(_WRAP) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_WRAP))\n\nimport operand_multihop3 as mh3  # noqa: E402\nimport writeback_compile as wb  # noqa: E402  (module reuse, no fork)\nfrom holo_frag import _json_safe  # noqa: E402\n\nfrom verbum.dsp import gate, paired_permutation  # noqa: E402\n\nSPLITS = wb.SPLITS\nLOWER_FRAC = 2.0 / 3.0     # L* selection band: lower 2/3 of the stack (frozen)\nN_LABEL_PERM = 2000        # scan null: permuted-label draws (frozen)\n\n\ndef unit(v: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    n = np.linalg.norm(v)\n    return v / n if n > 1e-12 else v\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Shared-Sigma multi-class key builder (the routing_register build_keys pattern,\n# multi-layer reusable): pop = all owns + innocents, single mu/cov, per-country\n# k_c = Sigma^-1(mu_own_c - mu). Returns (mu, keys{c: k, ref, inn_max, proto}).\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef build_keys_shared(owns: dict[str, np.ndarray], inn: np.ndarray, eps: float):\n    pop = np.vstack([*owns.values(), inn])\n    mu = pop.mean(axis=0)\n    xc = pop - mu\n    cov = (xc.T @ xc) / max(len(pop) - 1, 1)\n    d = cov.shape[0]\n    cov += eps * (np.trace(cov) / d) * np.eye(d)\n    keys = {}\n    for c, own in owns.items():\n        k = unit(np.linalg.solve(cov, own.mean(axis=0) - mu))\n        proj_own = (own - mu) @ k\n        proj_inn = (inn - mu) @ k\n        keys[c] = {\"k\": k.astype(np.float32),\n                   \"ref\": float(proj_own.mean()),\n                   \"inn_max\": float(proj_inn.max()),\n                   \"proto\": unit(own.mean(axis=0) - mu).astype(np.float32)}\n    return mu.astype(np.float32), keys\n\n\ndef decodability(mu, keys, lm_acts: np.ndarray, true_idx: np.ndarray,\n                 countries: list[str]) -> tuple[float, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"argmax-over-keys recognition accuracy of landmark activations. Returns\n    (acc, pred_idx). pred is FIXED wrt any label permutation (keys are name-built).\"\"\"\n    kmat = np.stack([keys[c][\"k\"] for c in countries])       # (C, D)\n    pred = ((lm_acts - mu) @ kmat.T).argmax(axis=1)          # (n,)\n    return float(np.mean(pred == true_idx)), pred\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Frozen scoring + verdict (pure; --validate exercises planted worlds)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef _g(a, b, rng, alpha, name):\n    a = np.asarray(a, float)\n    b = np.asarray(b, float)\n    return gate(float(np.mean(a - b)), paired_permutation(a, b, rng),\n                \"greater\", alpha, name=name)\n\n\ndef score(acc: dict, ce: dict, gh: dict, rng, alpha: float) -> dict:\n    a3 = alpha / 3.0\n    arm, base = \"fast_plate\", \"base\"\n    r = {}\n    g1 = {}\n    for sp in (\"B1\", \"B2\"):\n        gg = _g(acc[arm][sp], acc[base][sp], rng, a3, f\"F1-{sp}\")\n        g1[sp] = {\"gate\": gg,\n                  \"flip\": bool(acc[arm][sp].mean() > acc[base][sp].mean())}\n    r[\"F1\"] = bool(all(g1[sp][\"gate\"].verdict and g1[sp][\"flip\"]\n                       for sp in (\"B1\", \"B2\")))\n    r[\"F1_detail\"] = g1\n    g2 = _g(acc[arm][\"B2\"], acc[\"construct_lookup\"][\"B2\"], rng, a3, \"F2-B2\")\n    r[\"F2\"] = bool(g2.verdict)\n    r[\"F2_detail\"] = g2\n    held = np.concatenate([acc[arm][\"B1\"], acc[arm][\"B2\"]])\n    held_s = np.concatenate([acc[\"fast_plate_shuffle\"][\"B1\"],\n                             acc[\"fast_plate_shuffle\"][\"B2\"]])\n    g3 = _g(held, held_s, rng, a3, \"F3-heldout\")\n    r[\"F3\"] = bool(g3.verdict)\n    r[\"F3_detail\"] = g3\n    # advisory (NOT a gate): collapse load-bearing? fast_plate vs static_reinject\n    held_st = np.concatenate([acc[\"static_reinject\"][\"B1\"],\n                              acc[\"static_reinject\"][\"B2\"]])\n    gc = _g(held, held_st, rng, alpha, \"collapse-heldout\")\n    r[\"collapse_delta\"] = float(held.mean() - held_st.mean())\n    r[\"collapse_sig\"] = bool(gc.verdict)\n    r[\"collapse_detail\"] = gc\n    ce_ok = ce[arm] <= ce[base] * 1.02\n    g_ok = gh[arm][0] >= gh[base][0] - 0.10\n    h_ok = gh[arm][1] >= gh[base][1] - 0.10\n    r[\"F5\"] = bool(ce_ok and g_ok and h_ok)\n    r[\"F5_detail\"] = {\"ce\": ce[arm], \"ce_base\": ce[base],\n                      \"g_acc\": gh[arm][0], \"h_acc\": gh[arm][1]}\n    r[\"held_up\"] = bool(held.mean() > np.concatenate(\n        [acc[base][\"B1\"], acc[base][\"B2\"]]).mean())\n    return r\n\n\ndef verdict_of(gate0_ok: bool, m_pass: bool, r: dict) -> str:\n    if not gate0_ok:\n        return \"VOID (gate-0)\"\n    if not m_pass:\n        return \"STILL-EXTERNAL-BY-MEASUREMENT\"\n    if not r[\"F5\"]:\n        return \"HOST-DAMAGED\"\n    if r[\"F1\"] and r[\"F2\"] and r[\"F3\"]:\n        return (\"FAST-PLATE-WIRES (+COLLAPSE-LOAD-BEARING)\" if r[\"collapse_sig\"]\n                else \"FAST-PLATE-WIRES (+GEOMETRY-SUFFICES)\")\n    if r[\"F1\"]:\n        return \"UNSPECIFIC\"\n    return \"FAST-PLATE-INERT\"\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# --validate (no model)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:\n    ok = True\n    print(\"── §P-FAST-PLATE --validate (no model) ──\")\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)\n    D = 128\n    countries = [f\"C{i}\" for i in range(8)]\n\n    # 1. shared-key build + decodability: planted-separable owns classify; a\n    #    random layer does not; the label-permutation null sits at chance.\n    protos = {c: unit(rng.normal(size=D)) for c in countries}\n    inn = rng.normal(size=(12, D)) * 0.3\n    owns_good = {c: protos[c][None, :] * 3.0 + rng.normal(size=(4, D)) * 0.2\n                 for c in countries}\n    mu_g, keys_g = build_keys_shared(owns_good, inn, 0.1)\n    # landmark acts near their own proto (materialized layer)\n    tl_true = np.repeat(np.arange(8), 2)\n    tl_acts = np.stack([protos[countries[i]] * 3.0 + rng.normal(size=D) * 0.3\n                        for i in tl_true])\n    acc_g, _ = decodability(mu_g, keys_g, tl_acts, tl_true, countries)\n    owns_rand = {c: rng.normal(size=(4, D)) for c in countries}\n    mu_r, keys_r = build_keys_shared(owns_rand, inn, 0.1)\n    acc_r, _ = decodability(mu_r, keys_r, rng.normal(size=(16, D)),\n                            tl_true, countries)\n    # permuted-label null on the good layer\n    null = np.array([np.mean(rng.permutation(tl_true)\n                             == decodability(mu_g, keys_g, tl_acts, tl_true,\n                                             countries)[1])\n                     for _ in range(300)])\n    m_gate = gate(acc_g, null, \"greater\", alpha, name=\"M\")\n    good = acc_g > 0.8 and acc_r < 0.5 and m_gate.verdict\n    print(f\"[V] scan: decodable acc {acc_g:.2f} random acc {acc_r:.2f} \"\n          f\"M(p={m_gate.p:.3f}) {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}\")\n    ok &= good\n\n    # 2. plate hook mechanics (tiny torch): fast fires+adds correct proto on an\n    #    on-country activation, is silent on an innocent; shuffle adds deranged;\n    #    static adds a softmax mix.\n    import torch\n    Kmat = torch.tensor(np.stack([keys_g[c][\"k\"] for c in countries]),\n                        dtype=torch.float32)\n    innmax = torch.tensor([keys_g[c][\"inn_max\"] for c in countries],\n                          dtype=torch.float32)\n    proto_t = torch.tensor(np.stack([keys_g[c][\"proto\"] for c in countries]),\n                           dtype=torch.float32)\n    mu_t = torch.tensor(mu_g, dtype=torch.float32)\n    S = 1.0\n\n    def apply(mode, a_np, proto_mat):\n        h = torch.tensor(a_np, dtype=torch.float32)[None, None, :]  # (1,1,D)\n        A = h[0].float()\n        P = (A - mu_t) @ Kmat.T\n        if mode == \"static\":\n            W = torch.softmax(P, dim=1)\n            delta = S * (W @ proto_mat)\n        else:\n            pmax, cstar = P.max(dim=1)\n            fired = pmax > innmax[cstar]\n            delta = S * proto_mat[cstar] * fired[:, None].float()\n        return (h[0] + delta).numpy()[0], delta\n\n    on = protos[countries[3]] * 3.0\n    out_fast, d_fast = apply(\"fast\", on, proto_t)\n    exp = on + S * keys_g[countries[3]][\"proto\"]\n    e_on = float(np.abs(out_fast - exp).max())\n    inn_vec = inn[0]\n    _, d_inn = apply(\"fast\", inn_vec, proto_t)\n    silent = float(np.abs(d_inn.numpy()).max())\n    derange = wb.derangement(countries, np.random.default_rng(2))\n    proto_shuf = torch.tensor(\n        np.stack([keys_g[derange[c]][\"proto\"] for c in countries]),\n        dtype=torch.float32)\n    out_shuf, _ = apply(\"shuf\", on, proto_shuf)\n    exp_s = on + S * keys_g[derange[countries[3]]][\"proto\"]\n    e_shuf = float(np.abs(out_shuf - exp_s).max())\n    _, d_stat = apply(\"static\", on, proto_t)\n    stat_ok = float(np.linalg.norm(d_stat.numpy())) > 0\n    good = e_on < 1e-4 and silent < 1e-6 and e_shuf < 1e-4 and stat_ok\n    print(f\"[V] plate: fast-err {e_on:.2e} innocent-silent {silent:.2e} \"\n          f\"shuffle-err {e_shuf:.2e} static-nonzero {stat_ok} \"\n          f\"{'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}\")\n    ok &= good\n\n    # 3. derangement no fixed point\n    d = wb.derangement(sorted(wb.BANK), np.random.default_rng(1))\n    good = all(k != x for k, x in d.items()) and set(d.values()) == set(wb.BANK)\n    print(f\"[V] derangement: {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}\")\n    ok &= good\n\n    # 4. S = median native column norm\n    w = rng.normal(size=(D, 32))\n    s = float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(w, axis=0)))\n    print(f\"[V] S median col-norm {s:.3f} {'OK' if s > 0 else 'FAIL'}\")\n    ok &= s > 0\n\n    # 5. verdict planted worlds\n    def world(name, want, m_pass, fp, base, shuf, stat, lookup,\n              ce_bad=False, gh_bad=False):\n        rngw = np.random.default_rng(hash(name) & 0xFFFF)\n\n        def arr(p, n=64):\n            return (rngw.random(n) < p).astype(float)\n\n        acc = {\n            \"base\": {\"TRAIN\": arr(base[0]), \"B1\": arr(base[1]),\n                     \"B2\": arr(base[2])},\n            \"fast_plate\": {\"TRAIN\": arr(fp[0]), \"B1\": arr(fp[1]),\n                           \"B2\": arr(fp[2])},\n            \"fast_plate_shuffle\": {\"TRAIN\": arr(shuf[0]), \"B1\": arr(shuf[1]),\n                                   \"B2\": arr(shuf[2])},\n            \"static_reinject\": {\"TRAIN\": arr(stat[0]), \"B1\": arr(stat[1]),\n                                \"B2\": arr(stat[2])},\n            \"construct_lookup\": {\"TRAIN\": arr(lookup[0]), \"B1\": arr(lookup[1]),\n                                 \"B2\": arr(lookup[2])},\n        }\n        ce = {a: (1.10 if (ce_bad and a == \"fast_plate\") else 1.0) for a in acc}\n        gh = {a: ((0.5, 0.5) if (gh_bad and a == \"fast_plate\")\n                  else (0.95, 0.95)) for a in acc}\n        r = score(acc, ce, gh, np.random.default_rng(3), alpha)\n        v = verdict_of(True, m_pass, r)\n        hit = want in v\n        print(f\"[V] {name}-world -> {v} (want {want}) {'OK' if hit else 'FAIL'}\")\n        return hit\n\n    ok &= world(\"still-external\", \"STILL-EXTERNAL-BY-MEASUREMENT\", False,\n                fp=(.2, .12, .3), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.2, .12, .3),\n                stat=(.2, .12, .3), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))\n    ok &= world(\"collapse\", \"COLLAPSE-LOAD-BEARING\", True,\n                fp=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.2, .12, .2),\n                stat=(.4, .3, .35), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))\n    ok &= world(\"geometry\", \"GEOMETRY-SUFFICES\", True,\n                fp=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.2, .12, .2),\n                stat=(.93, .90, .93), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))\n    ok &= world(\"inert\", \"FAST-PLATE-INERT\", True,\n                fp=(.2, .12, .3), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.2, .12, .28),\n                stat=(.2, .12, .3), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))\n    ok &= world(\"unspecific\", \"UNSPECIFIC\", True,\n                fp=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.9, .9, .92),\n                stat=(.4, .3, .35), lookup=(.27, .12, .35))\n    ok &= world(\"host-damaged\", \"HOST-DAMAGED\", True,\n                fp=(.95, .92, .95), base=(.2, .12, .3), shuf=(.2, .12, .2),\n                stat=(.4, .3, .35), lookup=(.27, .12, .35), ce_bad=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──\")\n    return 0 if ok else 1\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Model path\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef run_model(args) -> int:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\n    dev = (args.device if (args.device != \"mps\"\n                           or torch.backends.mps.is_available()) else \"cpu\")\n    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)\n    if tok.pad_token is None:\n        tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token\n    tok.padding_side = \"left\"\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype)).to(dev).eval()\n    for p in model.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad_(False)\n    dec, _norm, lm_head = mh3.resolve_parts(model)\n    n_layers = len(dec)\n    out_dir = Path(args.out)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    rec = Path(args.record_dir)\n\n    def first_tid(w: str) -> int:\n        return mh3.first_tid(tok, w)\n\n    # ── valid cells + construct_lookup baseline from the FROZEN s303 record ──\n    g0 = json.loads((rec / \"gate0.json\").read_text())\n    gate0_ok = bool(g0[\"gate0_ok\"])\n    fields = (\"landmark\", \"city\", \"country\", \"capital\", \"split\")\n    valid = [wb.Cell(**{k: c[k] for k in fields}) for c in g0[\"cells\"]\n             if c.get(\"g_ok\") and c.get(\"h_ok\") and c.get(\"cot_ok\")]\n    ns = {sp: sum(1 for c in valid if c.split == sp) for sp in SPLITS}\n    res_frozen = json.loads((rec / \"results.json\").read_text())\n    lookup_b2 = {x[\"landmark\"]: x[\"correct\"]\n                 for x in res_frozen[\"arms\"][\"construct_lookup\"][\"seeds\"][0]\n                 if x[\"split\"] == \"B2\"}\n    print(f\"[fp] {args.model_id} dev={dev} n_layers={n_layers} valid={len(valid)} \"\n          f\"splits={ns} shuffle_seeds={args.seeds} gate0_ok={gate0_ok}\")\n\n    if args.n_cells:                       # smoke cap (mechanics only)\n        by = {sp: [c for c in valid if c.split == sp] for sp in SPLITS}\n        valid_eval = [c for sp in SPLITS for c in by[sp][:args.n_cells]]\n        lookup_b2 = {c.landmark: lookup_b2.get(c.landmark, 0.0)\n                     for c in valid_eval if c.split == \"B2\"}\n        print(f\"[fp] SMOKE cap {args.n_cells}/split -> {len(valid_eval)} cells\")\n    else:\n        valid_eval = valid\n\n    countries = sorted(wb.BANK)\n    caps = sorted({cap for cap, _ in wb.BANK.values()})\n\n    # ── union candidate set (rr pattern) ──\n    tid_map, drop = {}, set()\n    for w in wb.union_words():\n        t = first_tid(w)\n        clash = [x for x, tt in tid_map.items() if tt == t]\n        if clash:\n            drop.add(w)\n            drop.update(clash)\n        tid_map[w] = t\n    union = {w: tid_map[w] for w in sorted(set(wb.union_words()) - drop)}\n\n    def logits_last(prompt: str) -> np.ndarray:\n        ids = tok(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(dev)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            return model(**ids).logits[0, -1, :].float().cpu().numpy()\n\n    def argmax_union(lo):\n        return max(union, key=lambda w: lo[union[w]])\n\n    def margin(lo, truth):\n        return float(lo[union[truth]]\n                     - max(lo[union[w]] for w in union if w != truth))\n\n    def eval_cells() -> list[dict]:\n        rows = []\n        for c in valid_eval:\n            lo = logits_last(wb.DIRECT_PROMPT.format(lm=c.landmark))\n            arg = argmax_union(lo)\n            rows.append({\"landmark\": c.landmark, \"country\": c.country,\n                         \"split\": c.split, \"truth\": c.capital, \"arg\": arg,\n                         \"correct\": float(wb.first_word(arg)\n                                          == wb.first_word(c.capital)),\n                         \"margin\": margin(lo, c.capital),\n                         \"cap_logit\": float(lo[union[c.capital]])})\n        return rows\n\n    def ce_innocents() -> float:\n        tot, n = 0.0, 0\n        for t in wb.CE_TEXTS:\n            ids = tok(t, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(dev)\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                lo = model(**ids).logits\n            lp = F.log_softmax(lo[0, :-1].float(), dim=-1)\n            tgt = ids.input_ids[0, 1:]\n            tot += float(-lp[torch.arange(len(tgt)), tgt].sum())\n            n += len(tgt)\n        return tot / max(n, 1)\n\n    def gh_accs():\n        g = [max(countries, key=lambda w: logits_last(\n            wb.G_QUERY_PREFIX + wb.G_QUERY.format(lm=c.landmark))[first_tid(w)])\n            == c.country for c in valid_eval]\n        h = [wb.first_word(max(caps, key=lambda w: logits_last(\n            wb.CAP_PREFIX + wb.CAP_QUERY.format(x=co))[first_tid(w)]))\n            == wb.first_word(wb.BANK[co][0]) for co in sorted(wb.BANK)]\n        return float(np.mean(g)), float(np.mean(h))\n\n    # ── capture last-token residual at EVERY layer for a prompt list ──\n    def capture_all(prompts: list[str]) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]:\n        tmp: dict[int, np.ndarray] = {}\n        handles = [dec[L].register_forward_hook(mh3.cap_hook(tmp, L))\n                   for L in range(n_layers)]\n        acc = {L: [] for L in range(n_layers)}\n        for p in prompts:\n            ids = tok(p, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(dev)\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                model(**ids)\n            for L in range(n_layers):\n                acc[L].append(tmp[L][0, -1, :])\n        for h in handles:\n            h.remove()\n        return {L: np.stack(acc[L]) for L in range(n_layers)}\n\n    # ══ MATERIALIZATION SCAN (pre-gate M; TRAIN-only, no held peeking) ══\n    inn_prompts = list(wb.PROSE_INNOCENTS) + [\n        wb.DIRECT_PROMPT.format(lm=nc) for nc in wb.NONCE_CANDS[:3]]\n    name_prompts, name_owner = [], []\n    for c in countries:\n        for f in wb.CC_FRAMES:\n            name_prompts.append(f.format(x=c))\n            name_owner.append(c)\n    train_cells = [c for c in valid if c.split == \"TRAIN\"]  # full TRAIN (scan)\n    tl_prompts = [wb.DIRECT_PROMPT.format(lm=c.landmark) for c in train_cells]\n    tl_true = np.array([countries.index(c.country) for c in train_cells])\n\n    print(f\"[fp] scan: {len(name_prompts)} name frames, {len(inn_prompts)} \"\n          f\"innocents, {len(tl_prompts)} TRAIN landmarks across {n_layers} layers\")\n    name_caps = capture_all(name_prompts)\n    inn_caps = capture_all(inn_prompts)\n    tl_caps = capture_all(tl_prompts)\n\n    cand_layers = [L for L in range(n_layers) if L <= round(LOWER_FRAC * n_layers)]\n    dec_curve, preds_by_L, keys_by_L, mu_by_L = args.whiten_eps)\n        acc_L, pred_L = decodability(mu_L, keys_L, tl_caps[L], tl_true, countries)\n        dec_curve[L] = acc_L\n        preds_by_L[L] = pred_L\n        keys_by_L[L] = keys_L\n == best_acc)\n    # multiple-comparison-safe null: per label-permutation, max acc over cands\n    rng_scan = np.random.default_rng(args.seed + 7)\n    null_max = []\n    for _ in range(N_LABEL_PERM):\n        perm = rng_scan.permutation(tl_true)\n        null_max.append(max(float(np.mean(preds_by_L[L] == perm))\n                            for L in cand_layers))\n    null_max range(len(countries)) if (tl_true == i).any()]))\n    print(f\"[fp] scan: L*={li} decodability={best_acc:.3f} (p={m_gate.p:.4f}) \"\n          f\"M={'PASS' if m_pass else 'FAIL'} mlp.down_proj.weight.float().cpu().numpy()\n    S = float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(dn_w, axis=0)))\n    keys, mu = keys_by_L[li], mu_by_L[li]\n    print(f\"[fp] register scale S = median native down col-norm at L{li} = {S:.4f}\")\n\n    # ── the in-forward plate hook (all positions, residual space) ──\n    kmat_t = torch.tensor(np.stack([keys[c][\"k\"] for c in countries]),\n dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)\n    proto_np = np.stack([keys[c][\"proto\"] for c in countries])\n\n    def make_hook(mode: str, proto_mat: np.ndarray):\n        proto_t = torch.tensor(proto_mat, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)\n\n        def hook(_m, _i, out):\n            h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out\n            A = h[0].float()                          # (seq, D)\n            P = (A - mu_t) @ kmat_t.T                 # (seq, C)\n            if mode == \"static\":\n                W = torch.softmax(P, dim=1)\n                delta = S * (W @ proto_t)\n            else: fired[:, None]\n            h[0].add_(delta.to(h.dtype))\n            return out\n        return hook\n\n    def run_arm(hook_fn):\n        hnd = dec[li].register_forward_hook(hook_fn) if hook_fn else None\n        rows = eval_cells()\n        ce = ce_innocents()\n        gh = gh_accs()\n        if hnd:\n            hnd.remove()\n        return rows, ce, gh\n\n    # ══ base always (anchor) ══\n    print(\"[fp] ── base ──\")\n    base_rows, base_ce, base_gh = run_arm(None)\n    for sp in SPLITS:\n        print(f\"    {sp}: acc \"\n              f\"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in base_rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}\")\n\n    arms = {\"base\": {\"seeds\": [base_rows], \"ce\": base_ce, \"gh\": base_gh},\n            \"construct_lookup\": {\"b2\": lookup_b2}}\n    stats = {\"S\": S, \"scan\": {\"F1\": False, \"F2\": False, \"F3\": False, \"F5\": True,\n             \"collapse_sig\": False, \"collapse_delta\": 0.0}\n        v = verdict_of(gate0_ok, m_pass, r)\n        print(f\"\\n[fp] ════ run_arm(make_hook(\"static\", proto_np))\n    for sp in SPLITS:\n        print(f\"    {sp}: acc \"\n              f\"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in st_rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}\")\n\n    print(f\"[fp] ── fast_plate_shuffle ({args.seeds} derangement seeds) ──\")\n    shuf_seed_rows, shuf_ce, shuf_gh = [], [], []\n    for s in range(args.seeds):\n        d = wb.derangement(countries, np.random.default_rng(1000 + s))\n        proto_shuf = np.stack([keys[d[c]][\"proto\"] for c in countries])\n        rows, ce, gh = run_arm(make_hook(\"fast\", proto_shuf))\n        shuf_seed_rows.append(rows)\n        shuf_ce.append(ce)\n        shuf_gh.append(gh)\n        for sp in SPLITS:\n            print(f\"    seed {s} {sp}: acc \"\n                  f\"{np.mean([r['correct'] for r in rows if r['split']==sp]):.3f}\")\n\n    # advisory: reinject_landed = mean correct-capital-logit shift on held-out\n    def held_cap(rows):\n        return np.array([r[\"cap_logit\"] for r in rows if r[\"split\"] in (\"B1\", \"B2\")])\n    reinject_landed = float(held_cap(fp_rows).mean() - held_cap(base_rows).mean())\n    print(f\"[fp] reinject_landed (held-out correct-capital logit shift) \"\n          f\"= {reinject_landed:.3f}\")\n\n    arms.update({\n        \"fast_plate\": {\"seeds\": [fp_rows], \"ce\": fp_ce, \"gh\": fp_gh},\n        \"static_reinject\": {\"seeds\": [st_rows], \"ce\": st_ce, \"gh\": st_gh},\n        \"fast_plate_shuffle\": {\"seeds\": shuf_seed_rows,\n                               \"ce\": float(np.mean(shuf_ce)),\n                               \"gh\": tuple(np.mean(shuf_gh, axis=0))},\n    })\n    stats[\"reinject_landed\"] = reinject_landed\n\n    # ══ frozen scoring ══\n    order = {sp: [c.landmark for c in valid_eval if c.split == sp]\n             for sp in SPLITS}\n\n    def acc_arrays(label) -> dict:\n        per = {}\n        for sp in SPLITS:\n            mat = []\n            for rows in arms[label][\"seeds\"]:\n                bym = {r[\"landmark\"]: r[\"correct\"] for r in rows\n                       if r[\"split\"] == sp}\n                mat.append([bym[lm] for lm in order[sp]])\n            per[sp] = np.mean(np.array(mat), axis=0)\n        return per\n\n    acc = {a: acc_arrays(a) for a in (\"base\", \"fast_plate\", \"static_reinject\",\n                                      \"fast_plate_shuffle\")}\n    acc[\"construct_lookup\"] = {\n        \"B2\": np.array([lookup_b2[lm] for lm in order[\"B2\"]]),\n        \"B1\": np.zeros(len(order[\"B1\"])),\n        \"TRAIN\": np.zeros(len(order[\"TRAIN\"])),\n    }\n    ce = {\"base\": base_ce, \"fast_plate\": fp_ce}\n    gh = {\"base\": base_gh, \"fast_plate\": fp_gh}\n    r = score(acc, ce, gh, np.random.default_rng(args.seed + 999), args.alpha)\n    v = verdict_of(gate0_ok, m_pass, r)\n\n    anchor = {sp: {a: float(acc[a][sp].mean())\n                   for a in (\"base\", \"fast_plate\", \"static_reinject\",\n                             \"fast_plate_shuffle\")} for sp in SPLITS}\n    stats[\"anchor\"] = anchor\n\n    print(f\"\\n[fp] ════ VERDICT: {v} ════\")\n    print(f\"  F1={r['F1']} F2={r['F2']} F3={r['F3']} F5={r['F5']} \"\n          f\"collapse_sig={r['collapse_sig']} (Δ={r['collapse_delta']:+.3f})\")\n    print(f\"  L*={li} decodability={best_acc:.3f} lm-name-cos={lm_name_cos:.3f} \"\n          f\"reinject_landed={reinject_landed:.3f} S={S:.3f}\")\n    for sp in SPLITS:\n        print(f\"  {sp}: base {anchor[sp]['base']:.3f} fast \"\n \"splits\": ns},\n               \"arms\": arms, \"scoring\": scoring}\n    (out_dir / \"results.json\").write_text(\n        json.dumps(_json_safe(_degate(payload)), indent=2))\n    print(f\"[fp] wrote {out_dir}/results.json\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _degate(o):\n    if is_dataclass(o) and not isinstance(o, type):\n        return asdict(o)\n    if isinstance(o, dict):\n        return {k: """verbum.dsp.nulls — the yardstick layer: null constructors + the gate.

L1: pure numpy. No torch, no I/O, no model, no experiment logic.

Structural yardstick (λ yardstick, by construction):
- you cannot obtain a p-value from this library without declaring BOTH the
  null (a NullDraws) and the predicted direction ('greater' | 'less') first;
- sign discipline is enforced by shape: a significant p with the WRONG sign is
  verdict=False, never flipped, never rescued;
- register tags (λ measure) are warning-only: a mismatch writes to the
  warnings field and stderr — it NEVER mutates value, p, or verdict inputs.

Constructors return draws + provenance; the caller computes the observed
statistic; gate() compares. Nothing here decides what an experiment means —
verdict semantics beyond pass/fail belong to the instrument (design decision 3).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

import numpy as np

__all__ = [
    "Gated",
    "NullDraws",
    "Register",
    "gate",
    "matched_random",
    "matched_range",
    "paired_permutation",
    "shuffled_label",
    "sign_flip",
]


class Register(Enum):
    """λ measure verbatim: name the register before you build the probe."""
    routing = "routing"        # crisp/discrete: attention patterns, head selection
    value = "value"            # continuous/graded: residual content, subspaces
    contrast = "contrast"      # dark-field / difference channels (Q/M)
    magnitude = "magnitude"    # norms, energies, doses
    spectral = "spectral"      # eigen/singular structure
    causal = "causal"          # intervention -> outcome


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NullDraws:
    """Draws from a declared null + provenance (recorded at construction)."""
    name: str
    draws: np.ndarray
    provenance: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        object.__setattr__(self, "draws", np.asarray(self.draws, dtype=float))
        if self.draws.size == 0:
            raise ValueError(f"null '{self.name}' produced zero draws")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Gated:
    """The only object that carries a p-value. warnings NEVER alter data."""
    name: str
    value: float
    null_name: str
    null_mean: float
    null_std: float
    n_draws: int
    predict: str
    alpha: float
    p: float
    sign_ok: bool
    verdict: bool
    warnings: tuple[str, ...] = ()


def gate(value: float, null: NullDraws, predict: str, alpha: float = 0.05,
         name: str = "", claim_register: Register | None = None,
         probe_register: Register | None = None) -> Gated:
    """Compare an observed statistic against a declared null, directionally.

    predict: 'greater' (value predicted above null) or 'less'. Mandatory —
    there is no two-sided option (a prediction has a sign; λ yardstick).
    p is the add-one permutation p in the PREDICTED direction.
    verdict = (p < alpha) AND sign_ok. Wrong-sign extremity is a failure,
    reported verbatim, never flipped."""
    if not isinstance(null, NullDraws):
        raise TypeError("gate() requires a declared NullDraws (no null, no p)")
    if predict not in ("greater", "less"):
        raise ValueError("predict must be 'greater' or 'less' (declared a priori)")
    draws = null.draws
    v = float(value)
    if predict == "greater":
        p = float((1 + np.sum(draws >= v)) / (1 + draws.size))
        sign_ok = v > float(draws.mean())
    else:
        p = float((1 + np.sum(draws <= v)) / (1 + draws.size))
        sign_ok = v < float(draws.mean())
    warnings: list[str] = []
    if claim_register is not None and probe_register is not None \
            and claim_register is not probe_register:
        w = (f"register mismatch: claim={claim_register.value} "
             f"probe={probe_register.value} (s206 scar — verify the probe "
             f"measures the claimed quantity)")
        warnings.append(w)
        print(f"[dsp.gate] WARNING {name}: {w}", file=sys.stderr)
    return Gated(
        name=name, value=v, null_name=null.name,
        null_mean=float(draws.mean()), null_std=float(draws.std()),
        n_draws=int(draws.size), predict=predict, alpha=float(alpha),
        p=p, sign_ok=sign_ok, verdict=bool(p < alpha and sign_ok),
        warnings=tuple(warnings),
    )


# ── constructors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def shuffled_label(stat, y: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator,
                   n_iter: int = 200) -> NullDraws:
    """Full shuffled-label pipeline null: stat(permuted labels), n_iter times.

    stat: callable(label_array) -> float. The stat must RERUN the whole
    downstream pipeline on the shuffled labels (the QK lesson: shuffle ->
    centroids -> subspace -> same mapping -> same statistic), not just
    re-score cached intermediates. NaN draws are dropped (recorded)."""
    draws = []
    for _ in range(n_iter):
        v = float(stat(rng.permutation(y)))
        if not np.isnan(v):
            draws.append(v)
    return NullDraws("shuffled_label", np.array(draws),
                     {"n_iter": n_iter, "n_kept": len(draws)})


def matched_random(stat, dim: int, norm: float, rng: np.random.Generator,
                   n_iter: int = 200) -> NullDraws:
    """Matched-norm random-direction null: stat(random unit vector * norm).

    The exact 3b/P-ATT-MED null family: same norm, isotropic direction."""
    draws = []
    for _ in range(n_iter):
        v = rng.standard_normal(dim)
        v *= norm / (np.linalg.norm(v) + 1e-12)
        draws.append(float(stat(v)))
    return NullDraws("matched_random", np.array(draws),
                     {"n_iter": n_iter, "dim": dim, "norm": float(norm)})


def paired_permutation(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator,
                       n_iter: int = 10000) -> NullDraws:
    """Paired sign-flip permutation null over mean(a - b) (paired by index).

    The P-TYPE-SWAP s288 arm-vs-arm statistic. Observed value = mean(a - b),
    computed by the caller; draws = mean under random per-pair sign flips."""
    diffs = np.asarray(a, dtype=float) - np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    signs = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0]), size=(n_iter, diffs.size))
    draws = (signs * diffs[None, :]).mean(axis=1)
    return NullDraws("paired_permutation", draws,
                     {"n_iter": n_iter, "n_pairs": int(diffs.size)})


def sign_flip(values: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator,
              n_iter: int = 10000) -> NullDraws:
    """One-sample sign-flip null over mean(values) (H0: symmetric about 0).

    The 1c residual-sign discipline. Observed value = mean(values)."""
    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    signs = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0]), size=(n_iter, v.size))
    draws = (signs * v[None, :]).mean(axis=1)
    return NullDraws("sign_flip", draws,
                     {"n_iter": n_iter, "n": int(v.size)})


def matched_range(stat, target: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator,
                  n_iter: int = 200) -> NullDraws:
    """Matched-range null for geometric/spectral fits (λ yardstick MANDATORY
    gate for any approximate fit claim): stat(uniform draws over the target's
    observed range, same shape). If random values in the same range fit as
    well, describability != discovery (the s247 φ-ladder lesson)."""
    t = np.asarray(target, dtype=float)
    lo, hi = float(t.min()), float(t.max())
    draws = []
    for _ in range(n_iter):
        draws.append(float(stat(rng.uniform(lo, hi, size=t.shape))))
    return NullDraws("matched_range", np.array(draws),
                     {"n_iter": n_iter, "lo": lo, "hi": hi,
                      "shape": list(t.shape)})
```

I need to wrap the scan null array—which contains the max accuracies across permutations—in a NullDraws object. The cleanest approach is to import NullDraws and construct it directly, using the shuffled_label constructor with the statistic function that computes the maximum accuracy across candidate layers for each permuted label set.

Defining a stat closure and using shuffled_label is the idiomatic approach here. I'll refactor both run_model and run_validate to follow this pattern: create a stat function that takes permuted labels and returns the relevant accuracy metric, then pass it to shuffled_label along with the true labels, RNG, and iteration count to generate the null distribution. This gives me the multiple-comparison-safe null that the library expects.

### Thinking

The test run completed successfully with the FAST-PLATE-INERT verdict. Looking at the results, the fast variant significantly outperformed the static baseline with a negative delta of -0.026, and the landmark geometry shows strong decodability at 0.933 with the country properly materialized, though the landmark naming alignment came back slightly negative. The name proto reinject is geometrically misaligned with what the model reads from landmark context, and collapsing actually degrades performance compared to the soft always-on write. So the picture is now complete: every construction pathway—construct, routing_write, and fast_plate—is inert, while only gradient produces the wire. The key insight is that M passed (the country is linearly decodable), but the static read-collapse-reinject pipeline still fails, meaning the problem isn't that the intermediate doesn't exist—it's that you can't operationalize it through hand-written operations. So the country IS decodable at L24 (0.93) in the whitened discriminant geometry, even though it's absent in the L23 named geometry — it's materialized but in a different place and representation than routing_write expected. This sharpens the mechanism: not that the country is missing, but that it's present in the wrong geometric space for the routing to work. The failures of routing_write and fast_plate still stand, but now I see it's a geometry mismatch problem rather than pure absence.

Now I need to verify the run completed cleanly, then commit the results and log, draft the findings on the fast_plate page, prepare a memory candidate, update the state block with the verdict, and batch everything for Michael's approval on the synthesis.
